=== PROMPT VARIANT ===
r1

=== PROMPT ===
You will be given a competitive programming problem.
You are given a sequence A = (A_1, A_2, \dots, A_N) of length N.
Answer Q queries. The i-th query (1 \leq i \leq Q) is as follows:

- You are given integers R_i and X_i. Consider a subsequence (not necessarily contiguous) of (A_1, A_2, \dots, A_{R_i}) that is strictly increasing and consists only of elements at most X_i. Find the maximum possible length of such a subsequence.
It is guaranteed that X_i \geq \min\lbrace A_1, A_2,\dots,A_{R_i} \rbrace.

Input

The input is given from Standard Input in the following format:
N Q
A_1 A_2 \dots A_N
R_1 X_1
R_2 X_2
\vdots
R_Q X_Q

Output

Print Q lines. The i-th line should contain the answer to the i-th query.

Constraints


- 1 \leq N,Q \leq 2 \times 10^5
- 1 \leq A_i \leq 10^9
- 1 \leq R_i \leq N
- \min\lbrace A_1, A_2,\dots,A_{R_i} \rbrace\leq X_i\leq 10^9
- All input values are integers.

Sample Input 1

5 3
2 4 1 3 3
2 5
5 2
5 3

Sample Output 1

2
1
2


- 1st query: For the sequence (2,4), the longest strictly increasing subsequence with all elements at most 5 has length 2. Specifically, (2,4) qualifies.
- 2nd query: For the sequence (2,4,1,3,3), the longest strictly increasing subsequence with all elements at most 2 has length 1. Specifically, (2) and (1) qualify.
- 3rd query: For the sequence (2,4,1,3,3), the longest strictly increasing subsequence with all elements at most 3 has length 2. Specifically, (2,3) and (1,3) qualify.

Sample Input 2

10 8
2 5 6 5 2 1 7 9 7 2
7 8
5 2
2 3
2 6
7 3
8 9
9 6
8 7

Sample Output 2

4
1
1
2
1
5
3
4

Reason in a commit-implement-recheck way.

A productive rhythm for this task usually looks like this:
- Isolate the decisive constraints, the target complexity, and at most one or two approaches that are still truly plausible.
- If two candidates remain, compare them only on the decisive constraint and commit quickly to the stronger one instead of keeping a wide search open.
- Before coding, keep only a tiny implementation ledger for the chosen plan: main invariant, key state or transition, and the one or two edge cases most likely to fail.
- Move into code as soon as that ledger is clear. After coding starts, keep reasoning attached to the current draft rather than reopening the high-level search.
- After the first complete draft, do one short implementation-check-adjust-check loop on the same draft: test the key edge case, patch locally if needed, and recheck.
- Reopen the main design only if a concrete counterexample breaks the core invariant. Otherwise prefer the local patch and keep closing.
- Once the current draft, invariant, and short recheck still agree, finalize the solution.

Try to avoid patterns like these:
- spending too long comparing nearby plans before committing to one workable approach;
- returning from code to broad algorithm discussion without a concrete contradiction;
- turning one local doubt into a full restart when the current draft only needs a small patch.

For a sequence, LIS length ending at position i (or value) = 1 + max LIS length ending at previous positions with value < A_i. Standard online LIS with Fenwick over values: when processing A_i, val = 1 + max_{value < A_i} dp[value]; update dp[A_i]=max(dp[A_i], val). Answer LIS length = max dp. For prefix R, after processing first R, answer for X is max_{value <= X} dp[value]? Is that true? dp[v] = length of longest increasing subsequence in prefix ending with value exactly v (last element value v). Then any LIS with max value <= X has last value <= X, so length = dp[last value] <= max_{v<=X} dp[v]. Conversely dp[v] is length of some increasing subseq ending with v <= X, so valid. Thus answer = prefix maximum over values <= X of dp after processing first R. Great.

So we need support online processing positions:
- For each value a, maintain dp[a] = max LIS length ending with value a in processed prefix.
- When adding new element with value a, new_dp = 1 + max_{value < a} dp[value]. Then dp[a] = max(dp[a], new_dp). Because ending with same value can be updated by subsequence ending with lower value. Since strict increasing, equal not allowed. Duplicates: dp[a] should be max over all subsequences ending with value a; adding new a can create subsequence ending at this new occurrence with length 1+max lower. It may be > existing dp[a]. Update.
- Query after R: max_{value <= X} dp[value].

Need answer Q offline by R. We can process positions increasing, update data structure, answer queries at that R. Need data structure supports:
- point update: dp[a] = max(dp[a], value) for value a.
- prefix max query: max_{v <= X} dp[v].
- range max query for values < a to compute new_dp.
This is just Fenwick tree over compressed values supporting prefix max and point update max. Because query max over values < a = prefix max at coordinate a-1. Update at coordinate a with new_dp. Query answer prefix max at X. Complexity O((N+Q) log N). Is it that simple? Let's test sample.

Need implement FenwickMax:
class BIT:
  n
  tree=[0]*(n+1)
  update(i, val): while i<=n: if val>tree[i]: tree[i]=val; i += i&-i
  query(i): res=0; while i>0: res=max(res, tree[i]); i -= i&-i; return res
Since point update with max and prefix max. Is Fenwick max with point update and prefix query correct for arbitrary point updates? Implementation:
import sys, bisect
def main():
 data=list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
 it=iter(data)
 N=next(it); Q=next(it)
 A=[next(it) for _ in range(N)]
 vals=sorted(set(A))
 queries=[[] for _ in range(N+1)]
 for qi in range(Q):
    R=next(it); X=next(it)
    queries[R].append((X, qi))
 ans=[0]*Q
 bit=[0]*(len(vals)+1)
 def update(i, val):
    n=len(vals)
    while i<=n:
       if val>bit[i]: bit[i]=val
       i += i & -i
 def query(i):
    res=0
    while i>0:
       if bit[i]>res: res=bit[i]
       i -= i & -i
    return res
 for R in range(1,N+1):
    a=A[R-1]
    idx=bisect.bisect_left(vals, a)+1
    best=query(idx-1)+1
    update(idx, best)
    for X, qi in queries[R]:
       k=bisect.bisect_right(vals, X)
       ans[qi]=query(k)
 print('\n'.join(map(str, ans)))

Now, let's think if there is any issue with using Fenwick max for prefix max when updating at index i with value val. The standard Fenwick tree for prefix max:
- update(i, val): while i <= n: bit[i] = max(bit[i], val); i += i & -i
- query(i): res = 0; while i > 0: res = max(res, bit[i]); i -= i & -i
This works for prefix max if updates are point updates that only increase values. Let's verify with an example:
n=4. update(2,5): bit[2]=5, bit[4]=5. query(3): i=3 res=bit[3]=0, i=2 res=max(0,bit[2]=5)=5, i=0. query(3) should max index1..3 =5. ok.
update(3,7): bit[3]=7, bit[4]=max(5,7)=7. query(3): res=bit[3]=7, i=2 res=max(7,bit[2]=5)=7. ok.
query(2): i=2 res=bit[2]=5, i=0. ok.
update(1,6): bit[1]=6, bit[2]=max(5,6)=6, bit[4]=max(7,6)=7. query(2): i=2 bit[2]=6, i=0. max index1..2=6. ok. query(3): i=3 bit[3]=7, i=2 bit[2]=6 ->7. max index1..3=7. ok.
Works.

Let's maybe write final code:

```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = [[] for _ in range(N + 1)]
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries[R].append((X, qi))

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        for X, qi in queries[R]:
            k = br(vals, X)
            ans[qi] = query(k)

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

Let's maybe think about if there is a possible hidden challenge: The query asks for subsequence of prefix R with all elements <=X. We answer by max dp[v] for v<=X. Thus any subsequence counted in dp[v] is valid for threshold X. Conversely, any valid subsequence for threshold X has last value v<=X and length <=dp[v]. So max dp[v] over v<=X is exact. This is the key. Good.

Now, let's maybe think about if there is any issue with "Fenwick tree" and "point update max" if update at index i with val and later query prefix i includes val; yes. Let's prove Fenwick max:
- `bit[j]` stores max over range `(j - lowbit(j) + 1, j)`.
- Update at i: for each j such that i in range of j, update bit[j]. These j are i, i+lowbit(i), ... This is standard.
- Query prefix i: decompose [1,i] into disjoint Fenwick ranges by subtracting lowbit; max of bit[j]. Standard.
Works for max.

It adds sort but reduces memory a bit. Which code is less likely to have bug? List-of-lists no sort, but empty lists memory. Sorted queries requires while loop; if not careful, could miss queries if R not processed? Let's write sorted queries carefully:

```
queries = []
for qi in range(Q):
    R, X = map(int, input().split())
    queries.append((R, X, qi))
queries.sort()

ptr = 0
for R in range(1, N + 1):
    a = A[R - 1]
    idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
    best = query(idx - 1) + 1
    update(idx, best)
    while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
        _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
        ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
        ptr += 1
```
Since queries sorted by R, after processing R, ptr points to first query with R > R. If there are no queries for some R, while false. At the end, ptr should be Q. Good.
Potential issue: If queries sorted by tuple, for same R, sorted by X then qi; while processes all same R. Good.
This avoids creating N+1 empty lists. It also avoids storing queries in list-of-lists. I'll use this maybe. It is still simple.

Memory comparison: sorted queries stores R ints; list-of-lists doesn't store R ints but stores empty lists. Which is better? Sorted queries maybe slightly less memory? Let's calculate more accurately:
List-of-lists:
- outer list: (N+1)*8 = 1.6 MB
- empty lists: 200001*56 = 11.2 MB
- tuples (X, qi): 200k*56 = 11.2 MB
- X ints: 200k*28 = 5.6 MB
- qi ints: 200k*28 = 5.6 MB
Total query storage = 35.2 MB.
Sorted:
- queries list: 200k*8 = 1.6 MB
- tuples (R,X,qi): 200k*64 = 12.8 MB
- R ints: 200k*28 = 5.6 MB
- X ints: 5.6 MB
- qi ints: 5.6 MB
Total = 31.2 MB.
Saves 4 MB. Not huge. Sorting time. Both okay. Sorted queries also avoids creating many empty lists, which can be time. I'll use sorted queries for memory and no huge empty list overhead. But list-of-lists is more straightforward. The final code with sorted queries is still clear.

```
def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
```

```
def query(i, bit=bit):
    res = 0
    while i > 0:
        if bit[i] > res:
            res = bit[i]
        i -= i & -i
    return res
```
Good.

Now, let's think about if there is any issue with "ans" list and `map(str, ans)` if ans contains ints; okay.

Now, let's consider if there is any issue with "sys.stdout.write" if output string large; okay.

Now, let's think about if there is any issue with "main()" and `if __name__`; okay.

Now, let's decide final code with sorted queries. Let's write it cleanly:

```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

R=2 a=4 idx=4 best=query3+1. query3 max dp1=0 dp2=1 dp3=0 =>1 best2 update. while queries[0] R=2: X=5 br=4 query4=2 ans0=2 ptr1.
R=3 a=1 idx=1 best=1 update. no query R=3.
R=4 a=3 idx=3 best=query2 max dp1=1 dp2=1 =>1+1=2 update. no query R=4.
R=5 a=3 idx=3 best=query2=1+1=2 update. while queries[1] R=5 X=2 br=2 query2=1 ans1=1 ptr2; queries[2] R=5 X=3 br=3 query3=2 ans2=2. Output 2,1,2. Good.

```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```
```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

Now, let's decide final code with sorted queries. Let's write it cleanly:

```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

Let's test sample 1 with sorted queries:

R=1 a=2 idx=2 best=1 update. ptr query[0] R=2 no.
R=2 a=4 idx=4 best=query3+1. query3 max dp1=0 dp2=1 dp3=0 =>1 best2 update. while queries[0] R=2: X=5 br=4 query4=2 ans0=2 ptr1.
R=3 a=1 idx=1 best=1 update. no query R=3.
R=4 a=3 idx=3 best=query2 max dp1=1 dp2=1 =>1+1=2 update. no query R=4.
R=5 a=3 idx=3 best=query2=1+1=2 update. while queries[1] R=5 X=2 br=2 query2=1 ans1=1 ptr2; queries[2] R=5 X=3 br=3 query3=2 ans2=2. Output 2,1,2. Good.

```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

Now, let's consider if there is any issue with "elements at most X_i" and "X_i >= min" if X_i is 1e9 and A_i 1e9; okay.

Now, let's think about if there is any issue with "A_i" up to 1e9 and "X_i" up to 1e9; no.

Now, let's think about if there is any issue with "N,Q" up to 2e5 and recursion no.

Now, let's think about if there is any issue with "Fenwick tree" and "point update max" if values are not monotonic? dp values monotonic, but even if not, update with max is okay for point update if we only ever increase. We only increase. Good.

Now, let's consider if there is any issue with "query(idx - 1)" if there are values less than a but their dp values are from subsequences that use a? impossible. Good.

Now, let's think about if there is any issue with "queries" sorted and if there are queries with R equal to current but sorted by X; while processes all. Good.

Now, let's think about if there is any issue with "ans" list and output order; yes.

Now, let's consider if there is any issue with "A" list and "vals" if A has values not unique; set reduces. Good.

Now, let's think about if there is any issue with "bisect_left(vals, a)" if a is int and vals list of ints; okay.

Now, let's think about if there is any issue with "query(br(vals, X))" if X is int and vals list; okay.

Now, let's think about if there is any issue with "from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right" and using `bl` and `br` local; okay.

Now, let's consider if there is any issue with "update" function if `val` is 0? no.

Now, let's think about if there is any issue with "query" function if `i` is 0; returns 0. Good.

Now, let's think about if there is any issue with "bit" list and default argument `bit=bit`; if bit list is mutated, default reference sees mutations. Good.

Now, let's consider if there is any issue with "m = len(vals)" and update while i <= m; if m=0? no.

Now, let's think about if there is any issue with "queries" list and sorting if Q=2e5; okay.

Now, let's think about if there is any issue with "ans" list and `map(str, ans)` if ans contains ints; okay.

Now, let's consider if there is any issue with "sys.stdout.write" if output string large; okay.

Now, let's think about if there is any issue with "main()" and `if __name__`; okay.

Now, let's decide final code with sorted queries. Let's write it cleanly:

```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

R=3 a=1 idx=1 best=1 update. no query R=3.
R=4 a=3 idx=3 best=query2 max dp1=1 dp2=1 =>1+1=2 update. no query R=4.
R=5 a=3 idx=3 best=query2=1+1=2 update. while queries[1] R=5 X=2 br=2 query2=1 ans1=1 ptr2; queries[2] R=5 X=3 br=3 query3=2 ans2=2. Output 2,1,2. Good.

```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```
```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

Now, let's decide final code with sorted queries. Let's write it cleanly:

```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

```
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
```

Now, let's decide final code with sorted queries. Let's write it cleanly:

```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

Let's test sample 1 with sorted queries:

R=2 a=4 idx=4 best=query3+1. query3 max dp1=0 dp2=1 dp3=0 =>1 best2 update. while queries[0] R=2: X=5 br=4 query4=2 ans0=2 ptr1.
R=3 a=1 idx=1 best=1 update. no query R=3.
R=4 a=3 idx=3 best=query2 max dp1=1 dp2=1 =>1+1=2 update. no query R=4.
R=5 a=3 idx=3 best=query2=1+1=2 update. while queries[1] R=5 X=2 br=2 query2=1 ans1=1 ptr2; queries[2] R=5 X=3 br=3 query3=2 ans2=2. Output 2,1,2. Good.

```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```
```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

Now, let's decide final code with sorted queries. Let's write it cleanly:

```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

R=3 a=1 idx=1 best=1 update. no query R=3.
R=4 a=3 idx=3 best=query2 max dp1=1 dp2=1 =>1+1=2 update. no query R=4.
R=5 a=3 idx=3 best=query2=1+1=2 update. while queries[1] R=5 X=2 br=2 query2=1 ans1=1 ptr2; queries[2] R=5 X=3 br=3 query3=2 ans2=2. Output 2,1,2. Good.

```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```
```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

Now, let's decide final code with sorted queries. Let's write it cleanly:

```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

Now, let's decide final code with sorted queries. Let's write it cleanly:

```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

Let's test sample 1 with sorted queries:

R=2 a=4 idx=4 best=query3+1. query3 max dp1=0 dp2=1 dp3=0 =>1 best2 update. while queries[0] R=2: X=5 br=4 query4=2 ans0=2 ptr1.
R=3 a=1 idx=1 best=1 update. no query R=3.
R=4 a=3 idx=3 best=query2 max dp1=1 dp2=1 =>1+1=2 update. no query R=4.
R=5 a=3 idx=3 best=query2=1+1=2 update. while queries[1] R=5 X=2 br=2 query2=1 ans1=1 ptr2; queries[2] R=5 X=3 br=3 query3=2 ans2=2. Output 2,1,2. Good.

```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```
```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

Now, let's think about if there is any issue with "query" function if `i` is 0; returns 0. Good.

Now, let's think about if there is any issue with "bit" list and default argument `bit=bit`; if bit list is mutated, default reference sees mutations. Good.

Now, let's consider if there is any issue with "m = len(vals)" and update while i <= m; if m=0? no.

Now, let's think about if there is any issue with "queries" list and sorting if Q=2e5; okay.

Now, let's think about if there is any issue with "ans" list and `map(str, ans)` if ans contains ints; okay.

Now, let's consider if there is any issue with "sys.stdout.write" if output string large; okay.

Now, let's think about if there is any issue with "main()" and `if __name__`; okay.

Now, let's decide final code with sorted queries. Let's write it cleanly:

```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

R=3 a=1 idx=1 best=1 update. no query R=3.
R=4 a=3 idx=3 best=query2 max dp1=1 dp2=1 =>1+1=2 update. no query R=4.
R=5 a=3 idx=3 best=query2=1+1=2 update. while queries[1] R=5 X=2 br=2 query2=1 ans1=1 ptr2; queries[2] R=5 X=3 br=3 query3=2 ans2=2. Output 2,1,2. Good.

```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```
```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

R=4 a=3 idx=3 best=query2 max dp1=1 dp2=1 =>1+1=2 update. no query R=4.
R=5 a=3 idx=3 best=query2=1+1=2 update. while queries[1] R=5 X=2 br=2 query2=1 ans1=1 ptr2; queries[2] R=5 X=3 br=3 query3=2 ans2=2. Output 2,1,2. Good.

Now, let's decide final code with sorted queries. Let's write it cleanly:

```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

Let's test sample 1 with sorted queries:

R=3 a=1 idx=1 best=1 update. no query R=3.

```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```
```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

Now, let's think about if there is any issue with "dp[v]" and "answer = max dp[v] for v<=X" if the subsequence achieving dp[v] might not be valid because it uses elements after R? No, dp after processing R only. Good.

Now, let's decide final code with sorted queries. Let's write it cleanly:

```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

Let's test sample 1 with sorted queries:

R=2 a=4 idx=4 best=query3+1. query3 max dp1=0 dp2=1 dp3=0 =>1 best2 update. while queries[0] R=2: X=5 br=4 query4=2 ans0=2 ptr1.
R=3 a=1 idx=1 best=1 update. no query R=3.
R=4 a=3 idx=3 best=query2 max dp1=1 dp2=1 =>1+1=2 update. no query R=4.
R=5 a=3 idx=3 best=query2=1+1=2 update. while queries[1] R=5 X=2 br=2 query2=1 ans1=1 ptr2; queries[2] R=5 X=3 br=3 query3=2 ans2=2. Output 2,1,2. Good.

```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```
```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

Now, let's decide final code with sorted queries. Let's write it cleanly:

```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

R=2 a=4 idx=4 best=query3+1. query3 max dp1=0 dp2=1 dp3=0 =>1 best2 update. while queries[0] R=2: X=5 br=4 query4=2 ans0=2 ptr1.
R=3 a=1 idx=1 best=1 update. no query R=3.
R=4 a=3 idx=3 best=query2 max dp1=1 dp2=1 =>1+1=2 update. no query R=4.
R=5 a=3 idx=3 best=query2=1+1=2 update. while queries[1] R=5 X=2 br=2 query2=1 ans1=1 ptr2; queries[2] R=5 X=3 br=3 query3=2 ans2=2. Output 2,1,2. Good.

```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    main()
```

Now, let's decide final code with sorted queries. Let's write it cleanly:

```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

Let's test sample 1 with sorted queries:

R=2 a=4 idx=4 best=query3+1. query3 max dp1=0 dp2=1 dp3=0 =>1 best2 update. while queries[0] R=2: X=5 br=4 query4=2 ans0=2 ptr1.
R=3 a=1 idx=1 best=1 update. no query R=3.
R=4 a=3 idx=3 best=query2 max dp1=1 dp2=1 =>1+1=2 update. no query R=4.
R=5 a=3 idx=3 best=query2=1+1=2 update. while queries[1] R=5 X=2 br=2 query2=1 ans1=1 ptr2; queries[2] R=5 X=3 br=3 query3=2 ans2=2. Output 2,1,2. Good.

```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```
```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

Now, let's consider if there is any issue with "sys.stdout.write" if output string large; okay.

Now, let's think about if there is any issue with "main()" and `if __name__`; okay.

Now, let's decide final code with sorted queries. Let's write it cleanly:

```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```
```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

=== ANSWER ===
>
```python
import sys
from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, Q = map(int, input().split())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))

    vals = sorted(set(A))
    m = len(vals)

    queries = []
    for qi in range(Q):
        R, X = map(int, input().split())
        queries.append((R, X, qi))
    queries.sort()

    bit = [0] * (m + 1)

    def update(i, val, bit=bit, n=m):
        while i <= n:
            if bit[i] < val:
                bit[i] = val
            i += i & -i

    def query(i, bit=bit):
        res = 0
        while i > 0:
            if bit[i] > res:
                res = bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return res

    ans = [0] * Q
    bl = bisect_left
    br = bisect_right
    ptr = 0

    for R in range(1, N + 1):
        a = A[R - 1]
        idx = bl(vals, a) + 1
        best = query(idx - 1) + 1
        update(idx, best)

        while ptr < Q and queries[ptr][0] == R:
            _, X, qi = queries[ptr]
            ans[qi] = query(br(vals, X))
            ptr += 1

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```
